<a id="module-02"></a>
## 02 Route and current release authority

Hamish's 6 September 2026 release authorizes the sequential thirty-seat main-task cycle through v725-v8, one terminally gated edge at a time. Auren owns v687-v1 only. Future seat 07 owns v687-v2 only after a verified activation. Sable Rook v687-v3 is next after future seat 07's own terminal gate.

Before any mutation, the receiving seat must verify this committed baton, its index, Auren's exact final supplied in the compact activation, and Auren's external canonical receipt. Reuse an already created seat. Create no duplicate or substitute. Do not create a collaboration subagent for this route. Do not precontact Sable or another later owner during v687-v2.

The current release profile requires 200–500 inherited proposals, 200–500 new proposals, 300–500 safe tasks, 250–500 candidates, exactly 300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE entries, 50–250 exact packets, 30–100 blocked packets, at least ten built skills, at least five built runners, ten next-owner skill ideas, ten next-owner runner ideas, four own practice lenses, one successor recommendation, ten validated skill promotions, five shared runners, and three relevant isolated package additions for an ordinary bundle. Caps are not filler quotas.

GMUT Mind receives typed expectations, structural-delta distinctions, dependency closure, counterexample coverage, and hash-domain separation.
